Consider the space
\mathcal{Q}=\prod_{j=1}^{J}\mathcal{Q}_{j}
of vector valued functions
q(W)=\{q_{1}(W),...,q_{J}(W)\}
, where each component is almost surely bounded. Let
\ell\{W;q(W)\}
be a loss.

Lemma
\thechapter
.C.1

(Concentration; Lemma 14 of
Foster and Syrgkanis, 2023
)
.

Suppose Assumption
1
holds for each
\mathcal{Q}_{j}
. Further suppose
\ell
is
O(1)
Lipschitz in its second argument with respect to
\ell_{2}
norm. With probability
1-\zeta
, for any fixed
q_{0}\in\mathcal{Q}
independent of data and for all
q\in\mathcal{Q}
, when
\delta_{n}=\Omega[\{J\log\log(n)+\log(1/\zeta)\}^{1/2}n^{-1/2}]
,

\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)[\ell\{W;q(W)\}-\ell\{W;q_{0}(W)\}]\right|=O\left(J\delta_{n}\sum_{j=1}^{J}\left\|q_{j}-q_{0,j}\right\|_{2}+J\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

Lemma
\thechapter
.C.2

(High probability events)
.

Suppose Assumption
1
holds for
\mathcal{F}
,
\mathcal{G}
, and
\mathcal{H\times\mathcal{F}}
. With probability
1-\zeta
, when
\delta_{n}=\Omega[\{\log\log(n)+\log(1/\zeta)\}^{1/2}n^{-1/2}]
,

\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)[2\{h(B)-g(A)\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}]\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\left\|g-g_{0}\right\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

Proof.

We appeal to Lemma
\thechapter.C.1
for each term in the empirical process.

1.

Consider
\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{h(B)f(C)\}
. Let
q(W)=h(B)f(C)
,
q_{0}(W)=0
, and
\ell\{W,q(W)\}=h(B)f(C)
, which has derivative
1
in its second argument.
Then
\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{h(B)f(C)-0\}\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\|hf\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right)=O\left(\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right)
since
\mathcal{H}
is almost surely bounded.

2.

Consider
\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{g(A)f(C)\}
. Let
q(W)=\{g(A),f(C)\}
,
q_{0}(W)=\{g_{0}(A),0\}
, and
\ell\{W,q(W)\}=g(A)f(C)
, which has derivative
\{f(C),g(A)\}
in its second argument.
Then
\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{g(A)f(C)-0\}\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\|g-g_{0}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

3.

Consider
\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{f(C)^{2}\}
. Let
q(W)=f(C)
,
q_{0}(W)=0
, and
\ell\{W,q(W)\}=f(C)^{2}
, which has derivative
2f(C)
in its second argument.
Then
\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{f(C)^{2}-0\}\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).\qed

Lemma
\thechapter
.C.3

(High probability events under weaker conditions)
.

Suppose Assumption
1
holds for
\mathcal{F}
,
\mathcal{G}
, and
\mathcal{H}
. With probability
1-\zeta
, when
\delta_{n}=\Omega[\{\log\log(n)+\log(1/\zeta)\}^{1/2}n^{-1/2}]
, for a data independent hypothesis
h_{*}\in\mathcal{H}
,

\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)[2\{h(B)-g(A)\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}]\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\left\|h-h_{*}\right\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\left\|g-g_{0}\right\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

Proof.

The proof is identical to Lemma
\thechapter.C.2
except for the first empirical process
\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{h(B)f(C)\}
. Let
q(W)=\{h(B),f(C)\}
,
q_{0}(W)=\{h_{*}(B),0\}
, and
\ell\{W,q(W)\}=h(B)f(C)
, which has derivative
\{f(C),h(B)\}
in its second argument.
Then by Lemma
\thechapter.C.1
,
\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{h(B)f(C)-0\}\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\|h-h_{*}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

∎

Let
\|h\|^{2}_{2,n}=\mathbb{E}_{n}\{h(B)^{2}\}
,
I_{n}=2\mu(\|h\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|h_{*}\|_{2,n}^{2})
, and
I=2\mu(\|h\|^{2}_{2}-\|h_{*}\|_{2}^{2})
for some data independent
h_{*}\in\mathcal{H}
.

Lemma
\thechapter
.C.4

(High probability event for regularization)
.

Suppose Assumption
1
holds for
\mathcal{H}
. With probability
1-\zeta
, when
\delta_{n}=\Omega[\{\log\log(n)+\log(1/\zeta)\}^{1/2}n^{-1/2}]
, for a data independent hypothesis
h_{*}\in\mathcal{H}
,
|I_{n}-I|=O(\mu\delta_{n}\|h-h_{*}\|_{2}+\mu\delta_{n}^{2})
.

Proof.

Let
q(W)=h(B)
,
q_{0}(W)=h_{*}(B)
, and
\ell\{W,q(W)\}=h(B)^{2}
, which has derivative
2h(B)
in its second argument.
Then by Lemma
\thechapter.C.1
,
|\|h\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|h_{*}\|_{2,n}^{2}-(\|h\|^{2}_{2}-\|h_{*}\|_{2}^{2})|=\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{h(B)^{2}-h_{*}(B)^{2}\}\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\|h-h_{*}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

∎

\thechapter
.C.2
Adversarial maximization

Let
\omega>0
and define

L_{\omega}(h,g)=\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h(B)-g(A)\right\}f(C)-\omega f(C)^{2}\right]
. Let
f_{h}=T(h-h_{0},0)
and
f_{g}=T(0,g-g_{0})
so that
f_{h}+f_{g}=T(h-h_{0},g-g_{0})
.

Lemma
\thechapter
.C.5

(Maximization identity)
.

If
f_{h},f_{g}\in\mathcal{F}
for any
h\in\mathcal{H}
and
g\in\mathcal{G}
, then

L_{\omega}(h,g)=\omega^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h(B)-g(A)\right\}(f_{h}+f_{g})(C)-(f_{h}+f_{g})^{2}(C)\right]=\omega^{-1}\left\|T\left(h-h_{0},g-g_{0}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}.

In particular, since
\mathbb{E}[\{h_{0}(B)-g_{0}(A)\}f(C)]=0
,

\displaystyle L_{\omega}(h,g_{0})

\displaystyle=\omega^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h(B)-h_{0}(B)\right\}f_{h}(C)-f_{h}^{2}(C)\right]=\omega^{-1}\left\|T\left(h-h_{0},0\right)\right\|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle L_{\omega}(h_{0},g)

\displaystyle=\omega^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{g_{0}(A)-g(A)\right\}f_{g}(C)-f_{g}^{2}(C)\right]=\omega^{-1}\left\|T\left(0,g-g_{0}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}.

Proof.

By the law of iterated expectations, write
L_{\omega}(h,g)
equal to

\displaystyle\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h(B)-h_{0}(B)+g_{0}(A)-g(A)\right\}f(C)-\omega f(C)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left\{2\cdot T(h-h_{0},g-g_{0})f(C)\right\}-\omega\mathbb{E}\{f(C)^{2}\}=\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}2\langle T(h-h_{0},g-g_{0}),f\rangle_{2}-\omega\langle f,f\rangle_{2}.

Taking the Gateaux derivative with respect to
f
, we see that the first order condition is
2T(h-h_{0},g-g_{0})-2\omega f^{*}=0
. Rearranging,
f^{*}=\omega^{-1}T(h-h_{0},g-g_{0})
. Substitute
f^{*}
into initial and final expressions in the display, and recall
T(h-h_{0},g-g_{0})=f_{h}+f_{g}
.
∎

\thechapter
.C.3
Algorithm
1

We study the ridge regularized estimator and its population analogue:

\displaystyle\hat{h}

\displaystyle=\arg\min_{h\in\mathcal{H}}\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{h(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]+\mu\mathbb{E}_{n}\{h(B)^{2}\},

\displaystyle h_{\mu}

\displaystyle=\arg\min_{h\in\mathcal{H}}\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h(B)-g_{0}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]+\mu\mathbb{E}\{h(B)^{2}\}.

Lemma
\thechapter
.C.6

(From weak to strong metric)
.

For any
\mu>0
,

\|T(\hat{h}-h_{2\mu})\|^{2}_{2}+2\mu\|\hat{h}-h_{2\mu}\|^{2}_{2}=\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|^{2}_{2}-\|T(h_{2\mu}-h_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+2\mu(\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2}-\|h_{2\mu}\|^{2}_{2}).

Proof.

To lighten notation, let
h_{(\tau)}=h_{2\mu}+\tau(\hat{h}-h_{2\mu})
.
Define

W(\tau)=\|T\{h_{(\tau)}-h_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+2\mu\|h_{(\tau)}\|^{2}_{2}
. Clearly
W(\tau)
is quadratic in
\tau
and strongly convex. By Lemma
\thechapter.C.5
,

\displaystyle W(\tau)

\displaystyle=L_{1}\{h_{(\tau)},g_{0}\}+2\mu\|h_{(\tau)}\|^{2}_{2}=\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h_{(\tau)}(B)-g_{0}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]+2\mu\|h_{(\tau)}\|^{2}_{2}

which is minimized at
\tau=0
by the definition of
h_{2\mu}
. Therefore by an exact Taylor expansion,

\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}^{2}W(0)=\partial_{\tau}W(0)+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}^{2}W(0)=W(1)-W(0).

The derivatives of
W(\tau)
are

\partial_{\tau}W(\tau)=2\langle T\{h_{(\tau)}-h_{0}\},T(\hat{h}-h_{2\mu})\rangle_{2}+4\mu\langle h_{(\tau)},\hat{h}-h_{2\mu}\rangle_{2}

and

\partial_{\tau}^{2}W(\tau)=2\|T(\hat{h}-h_{2\mu})\|_{2}^{2}+4\mu\|\hat{h}-h_{2\mu}\|_{2}^{2}.

Substituting in
\partial_{\tau}^{2}W(0)
,
W(1)
, and
W(0)
into the Taylor expansion yields the result.
∎

Lemma
\thechapter
.C.7

(Relating weak metrics)
.

Suppose the conditions of Lemmas
\thechapter.C.5
and
\thechapter.C.2
hold. With probability
1-\zeta
, when
\delta_{n}=\Omega[\{\log\log(n)+\log(1/\zeta)\}^{1/2}n^{-1/2}]
,

\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}-\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}\leq 8\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+2\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.

Proof.

We proceed in steps.

1.

By Lemma
\thechapter.C.5
,

\displaystyle\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=L_{1}(\hat{h},g_{0})=\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-h_{0}(B)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)-f_{\hat{h}}(C)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)+\hat{g}(A)-g_{0}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)-f_{\hat{h}}(C)^{2}\right].

2.

Focusing on the third and fourth term, by Lemma
\thechapter.C.5
, Cauchy Schwarz, and AM-GM

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\hat{g}(A)-g_{0}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}\leq 2\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|^{2}_{2}.

3.

Focusing on the remaining terms, by Lemma
\thechapter.C.2
with probability
1-\zeta

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)-f_{\hat{h}}(C)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)-f_{\hat{h}}(C)^{2}\right]+O\left(\delta_{n}\left\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\right\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|f_{\hat{h}}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

(a)

For the empirical expectation, by Assumption
3
, the definition of
\hat{h}
, Lemma
\thechapter.C.2
, and the AM-GM inequality
O(\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2})\leq\frac{1}{2}\|f\|^{2}_{2}+O(\delta_{n}^{2})
, with probability
1-\zeta
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)-f_{\hat{h}}(C)^{2}\right]\leq\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{h_{*}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h_{*}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]+O\left(\delta_{n}\left\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\right\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h_{*}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-\frac{1}{2}f(C)^{2}\right]+O\left(\delta_{n}\left\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\right\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right)+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n}).

By triangle inequality, Lemma
\thechapter.C.5
, and Jensen’s inequality

\displaystyle\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h_{*}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-\frac{1}{2}f(C)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h_{*}(B)-g_{0}(A)\right\}f(C)-\frac{1}{4}f(C)^{2}\right]+\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h_{0}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-\frac{1}{4}f(C)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=L_{1/4}(h_{*},g_{0})+L_{1/4}(h_{0},\hat{g})=4\|T(h_{*}-h_{0},0)\|_{2}^{2}+4\|T(0,\hat{g}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+4\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}.

In summary,
\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)-f_{\hat{h}}(C)^{2}\right]
is bounded by

4\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+O\left(\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right)+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n}).

(b)

Consider the penultimate term.
By Lemma
\thechapter.C.5
, triangle inequality, and AM-GM,

\displaystyle O(\delta_{n}\|f_{\hat{h}}\|_{2})

\displaystyle=O\{\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}\}=O\{\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+O\{\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.

In summary, we bound
\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)-f_{\hat{h}}(C)^{2}\right]
by

\displaystyle\frac{9}{2}\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.

4.

Collecting results,

\displaystyle\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{9}{2}\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})

\displaystyle\quad+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.\qed

Lemma
\thechapter
.C.8

(Relating weak metrics under weaker conditions)
.

Suppose the conditions of Lemmas
\thechapter.C.5
and
\thechapter.C.3
hold. With probability
1-\zeta
, when
\delta_{n}=\Omega[\{\log\log(n)+\log(1/\zeta)\}^{1/2}n^{-1/2}]
,

\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}-\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}\leq 8\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+2\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|\hat{h}-h_{*}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}
.

Proof.

The argument is identical to Lemma
\thechapter.C.7
, using Lemma
\thechapter.C.3
instead of
\thechapter.C.2
.
∎

Lemma
\thechapter
.C.9

(Regularization bias; Lemma 3 of
Bennett et al., 2023c
)
.

If Assumption
2
holds then
\|h_{\mu}-h_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|w_{h}\|^{2}_{2}\mu^{\min(\beta,2)}
and
\|T(h_{\mu}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|w_{h}\|^{2}_{2}\mu^{\min(\beta+1,2)}
.

Proof.

For completeness, we present the proof in Appendix
\thechapter.E
.
∎

Proof of Theorem
1
.

We consolidate both versions of the result, either placing the stronger assumption on the product space (
\chi=0
, Lemma
\thechapter.C.2
) or not (
\chi=1
, Lemma
\thechapter.C.3
). Take
h_{*}=h_{2\mu}
.

1.

By Lemmas
\thechapter.C.6
,
\thechapter.C.7
,
\thechapter.C.8
, and
\thechapter.C.4
, AM-GM inequality, and
\mu+\chi=O(1)
, we bound the quantity
\|T(\hat{h}-h_{2\mu})\|^{2}_{2}+2\mu\|\hat{h}-h_{2\mu}\|^{2}_{2}
by

\displaystyle\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|^{2}_{2}-\|T(h_{2\mu}-h_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+2\mu(\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2}-\|h_{2\mu}\|^{2}_{2})

\displaystyle\leq 8\|T(h_{2\mu}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+2\mu(\|h_{2\mu}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})+2\mu(\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2}-\|h_{2\mu}\|^{2}_{2})

\displaystyle\quad+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{2\mu})\|_{2}+\chi\delta_{n}\|\hat{h}-h_{2\mu}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}

\displaystyle=8\|T(h_{2\mu}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}-I_{n}+I+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{2\mu})\|_{2}+\chi\delta_{n}\|\hat{h}-h_{2\mu}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}

\displaystyle\leq 8\|T(h_{2\mu}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{2\mu})\|_{2}+(\mu+\chi)\delta_{n}\|\hat{h}-h_{2\mu}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}

\displaystyle\leq 8\|T(h_{2\mu}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{2\mu})\|^{2}_{2}+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+(\sqrt{\mu+\chi})^{2}\delta_{n}\|\hat{h}-h_{2\mu}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}

\displaystyle\leq 8\|T(h_{2\mu}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{2\mu})\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{\mu+\chi}{2}\|\hat{h}-h_{2\mu}\|^{2}_{2}+O(\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}).

Rearranging yields

\frac{1}{2}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{2\mu})\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{3\mu-\chi}{2}\|\hat{h}-h_{2\mu}\|^{2}_{2}\leq 8\|T(h_{2\mu}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+O(\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}),

\displaystyle\text{ hence }\|T(\hat{h}-h_{2\mu})\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 16\|T(h_{2\mu}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+O(\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}),

\displaystyle\|\hat{h}-h_{2\mu}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16}{3\mu-\chi}\|T(h_{2\mu}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{2}{3\mu-\chi}\cdot O(\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}).

2.

For the weak metric, we use triangle inequality and Lemma
\thechapter.C.9
:

\displaystyle\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|T(\hat{h}-h_{2\mu})\|_{2}^{2}+2\|T(h_{2\mu}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}\leq 18\|T(h_{2\mu}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+O(\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}^{2})

\displaystyle=O\{\|w_{h}\|^{2}_{2}\mu^{\min(\beta+1,2)}+\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.

3.

For the strong metric, we use triangle inequality and Lemma
\thechapter.C.9
:

\displaystyle\|\hat{h}-h_{0}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\hat{h}-h_{2\mu}\|_{2}^{2}+2\|h_{2\mu}-h_{0}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{32}{3\mu-\chi}\|T(h_{2\mu}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+2\|h_{2\mu}-h_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{4}{3\mu-\chi}\cdot O(\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}^{2})

\displaystyle=O\left\{\frac{\|w_{h}\|^{2}_{2}\mu^{\min(\beta+1,2)}}{\mu-\chi/3}+\|w_{h}\|^{2}_{2}\mu^{\min(\beta,2)}+\frac{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}}{\mu-\chi/3}\right\}.

When
\chi=0
,
\|\hat{h}-h_{0}\|_{2}^{2}=\{\|w_{h}\|^{2}_{2}\mu^{\min(\beta,1)}+\mu^{-1}\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\mu^{-1}\delta_{n}^{2}\}
. ∎

\thechapter
.C.4
Algorithm
\thechapter.B.1

We now study

\hat{h}=\arg\min_{h\in\mathcal{H}}\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{h(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]-\mu^{\prime\prime}\left\|f\right\|_{\mathcal{F}}^{2}+\mu\left\|h\right\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}.

Lemma
\thechapter
.C.10

(Relating weak metrics)
.

Suppose the conditions of Lemma
\thechapter.C.7
hold and
\|T(h-h_{0})\|^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}\leq\textsc{lip}\|h-h_{0}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}
. With probability
1-\zeta
, when
\delta_{n}=\Omega[\{\log\log(n)+\log(1/\zeta)\}^{1/2}n^{-1/2}]
,

\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}-\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}\leq 8\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+2\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}})+2\mu^{\prime\prime}\textsc{lip}\|\hat{h}-h_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.

Proof.

We proceed in steps similar to Lemma
\thechapter.C.7
.

1.

As before,

\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}=\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)+\hat{g}(A)-g_{0}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)-f_{\hat{h}}(C)^{2}\right].

2.

As before,

\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\hat{g}(A)-g_{0}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)\right]\leq 2\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|^{2}_{2}.

3.

As before, with probability
1-\zeta
, we bound
\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)-f_{\hat{h}}(C)^{2}\right]
by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)-f_{\hat{h}}(C)^{2}\right]+O\left(\delta_{n}\left\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\right\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|f_{\hat{h}}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

(a)

Consider the empirical expectation. By Assumption
3
, the definition of
\hat{h}
, Lemma
\thechapter.C.2
, and the AM-GM inequality
O(\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2})\leq\frac{1}{2}\|f\|^{2}_{2}+O(\delta_{n}^{2})
, with probability
1-\zeta
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)-f_{\hat{h}}(C)^{2}\right]-\mu^{\prime\prime}\|f_{\hat{h}}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]-\mu^{\prime\prime}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{h_{*}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}})-\mu^{\prime\prime}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h_{*}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]+O\left(\delta_{n}\left\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\right\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}})-\mu^{\prime\prime}\|f\|^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h_{*}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-\frac{1}{2}f(C)^{2}\right]+O\left(\delta_{n}\left\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\right\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right)+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}).

As before,

\displaystyle\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h_{*}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-\frac{1}{2}f(C)^{2}\right]\leq 4\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+4\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}.

Moreover,

\|f_{\hat{h}}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}=\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|^{2}_{\mathcal{F}}\leq\textsc{lip}\|\hat{h}-h_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}.

We conclude that the quantity
\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)-f_{\hat{h}}(C)^{2}\right]
is bounded by

4\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+O\left(\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right)+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}})+\mu^{\prime\prime}\textsc{lip}\|\hat{h}-h_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}.

(b)

Consider the penultimate term.
As before,

O(\delta_{n}\|f_{\hat{h}}\|_{2})\leq\frac{1}{2}\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+O\{\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.

In summary, we bound
\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f_{\hat{h}}(C)-f_{\hat{h}}(C)^{2}\right]
by

\displaystyle\frac{9}{2}\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}})+\mu^{\prime\prime}\textsc{lip}\|\hat{h}-h_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.

4.

Collecting results,

\displaystyle\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{9}{2}\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}})+\mu^{\prime\prime}\textsc{lip}\|\hat{h}-h_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.

Finally, rearrange as before. ∎

Proof of Theorem
\thechapter.B.1
.

Take
h_{*}=h_{0}
. By Lemma
\thechapter.C.10
, we bound
\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}
by

2\mu(\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}})+2\mu^{\prime\prime}\textsc{lip}\|\hat{h}-h_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.

Since
2\mu^{\prime\prime}\textsc{lip}\|\hat{h}-h_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\leq 4\mu^{\prime\prime}\textsc{lip}(\|\hat{h}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}+\|h_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\leq 2\mu(\|\hat{h}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}+\|h_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}}^{2})
, we have

\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}\leq 4\mu\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.

By AM-GM inequality,

\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}\leq 4\mu\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{\mathcal{H}}+\frac{1}{2}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+O\{\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.\qed

Appendix
\thechapter
.D
Proof of Theorem
2

As before, let
S:g\mapsto\mathbb{E}\{g(A)|C^{\prime}=\cdot\}
and
T:(h,g)\mapsto\mathbb{E}\{h(B)-g(A)|C=\cdot\}
. Let
(h_{0},g_{0})
be the minimal
\mathbb{L}_{2}
norm solutions to
S(g)-Y=0
and
T(h,g)=0
.

\thechapter
.D.1
High probability events

Lemma
\thechapter
.D.1

(High probability events)
.

Suppose Assumption
1
holds for
\mathcal{F}
,
\mathcal{F}^{\prime}
,
\mathcal{G}\times\mathcal{F}
,

\mathcal{G}\times\mathcal{F}^{\prime}
, and
\mathcal{H}\times\mathcal{F}
. With probability
1-\zeta
, when
\delta_{n}=\Omega[\{\log\log(n)+\log(1/\zeta)\}^{1/2}n^{-1/2}]
,

\displaystyle\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)[2\{g(A)-Y\}f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})-f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})^{2}]\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\|f^{\prime}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)[2\{h(B)-g(A)\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}]\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

Proof.

We appeal to Lemma
\thechapter.C.1
for each term in the former empirical process, similar to Lemma
\thechapter.C.2
.

1.

Consider
\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{g(A)f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})\}
. Let
q(W)=g(A)f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})
,
q_{0}(W)=0
, and
\ell\{W,q(W)\}=g(A)f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})
, which has derivative
1
in its second argument.
Then
\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{g(A)f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})-0\}\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\|gf^{\prime}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right)=O\left(\delta_{n}\|f^{\prime}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right)
since
\mathcal{G}
is almost surely bounded.

2.

Consider
\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{Yf^{\prime}(C^{\prime})\}
. Let
q(W)=f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})
,
q_{0}(W)=0
, and
\ell\{W,q(W)\}=Yf^{\prime}(C^{\prime})
, which has derivative
Y
in its second argument.
Then
\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{Yf^{\prime}(C^{\prime})-0\}\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\|f^{\prime}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

3.

As before,
\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})^{2}-0\}\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\|f^{\prime}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

Next we turn to the latter empirical process.

1.

As before,
\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{h(B)f(C)-0\}\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

2.

Similarly,
\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{g(A)f(C)-0\}\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

3.

As before,
\left|\left(\mathbb{E}_{n}-\mathbb{E}\right)\{f(C)^{2}-0\}\right|=O\left(\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).\qed

Let
\|g\|^{2}_{2,n}=\mathbb{E}_{n}\{g(A)^{2}\}
,
I_{n}^{\prime}=2\mu^{\prime}(\|g\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|g_{*}\|_{2,n}^{2})
, and
I^{\prime}=2\mu^{\prime}(\|g\|^{2}_{2}-\|g_{*}\|_{2}^{2})
for some data independent
g_{*}\in\mathcal{G}
.

Lemma
\thechapter
.D.2

(High probability event for regularization)
.

Suppose Assumption
1
holds for
\mathcal{G}
. With probability
1-\zeta
, when
\delta_{n}=\Omega[\{\log\log(n)+\log(1/\zeta)\}^{1/2}n^{-1/2}]
, for a data independent hypothesis
g_{*}\in\mathcal{G}
,
|I^{\prime}_{n}-I^{\prime}|=O(\mu^{\prime}\delta_{n}\|g-g_{*}\|_{2}+\mu^{\prime}\delta_{n}^{2})
.

Proof.

The argument is identical to Lemma
\thechapter.C.4
.
∎

\thechapter
.D.2
Adversarial maximization

Let
\omega>0
,

L^{\prime}_{\omega}(g)=\sup_{f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{g(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})-\omega f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})^{2}\right]
, and
f^{\prime}_{g}=S(g-g_{0})
.

Lemma
\thechapter
.D.3

(Maximization identity)
.

If
f^{\prime}_{g}\in\mathcal{F}^{\prime}
for any
g\in\mathcal{G}
, then

L^{\prime}_{\omega}(g)=\omega^{-1}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{g(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}_{g}(C^{\prime})-(f^{\prime}_{g})^{2}(C^{\prime})\right]=\omega^{-1}\left\|S\left(g-g_{0}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}.

Proof.

By the law of iterated expectations,
L^{\prime}_{\omega}(g)
equals

\displaystyle\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{g(A)-g_{0}(A)\right\}f(C^{\prime})-\omega f(C^{\prime})^{2}\right]=\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\left\{2\cdot S(g-g_{0})f(C^{\prime})\right\}-\omega\mathbb{E}\{f(C^{\prime})^{2}\}

\displaystyle=\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}^{\prime}}2\langle S(g-g_{0}),f\rangle_{2}-\omega\langle f,f\rangle_{2}.

Taking the Gateaux derivative with respect to
f
, we see that the first order condition is
2S(g-g_{0})-2\omega f^{*}=0
. Rearranging,
f^{*}=\omega^{-1}S(g-g_{0})
. Substitute
f^{*}
into initial and final expressions in the display, and recall
S(g-g_{0})=f^{\prime}_{g}
.
∎

\thechapter
.D.3
Algorithm
2

We study Algorithm
2
and its population analogue:

\displaystyle(\hat{g},\hat{h})

\displaystyle=\arg\min_{g\in\mathcal{G},h\in\mathcal{H}}\sup_{f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{g(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})-f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})^{2}\right]+\mu^{\prime}\mathbb{E}_{n}\{g(A)^{2}\}

\displaystyle\quad+\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{h(B)-g(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]+\mu\mathbb{E}_{n}\{h(B)^{2}\},

\displaystyle\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}

\displaystyle=\arg\min_{g\in\mathcal{G},h\in\mathcal{H}}\sup_{f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{g(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})-f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})^{2}\right]+2\mu^{\prime}\mathbb{E}\{g(A)^{2}\}

\displaystyle\quad+\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h(B)-g(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]+2\mu\mathbb{E}\{h(B)^{2}\}.

Lemma
\thechapter
.D.4

(From weak to strong metric)
.

For any
\mu^{\prime},\mu>0
,

\displaystyle\|S\{\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}^{2}+2\mu^{\prime}\|\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}^{2}+\|T\{\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}^{2}+2\mu\|\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|S(\hat{g}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}-\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+2\mu^{\prime}\{\|\hat{g}\|^{2}_{2}-\|g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|^{2}_{2}\}

\displaystyle+\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0},\hat{g}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}-\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+2\mu\{\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2}-\|h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|^{2}_{2}\}.

Proof.

Let
g_{(\tau)}=g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}+\tau\{\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}
,
h_{(\tau)}=h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}+\tau\{\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}
, and

W(\tau)=\|S\{g_{(\tau)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+2\mu^{\prime}\|g_{(\tau)}\|^{2}_{2}+\|T\{h_{(\tau)}-h_{0},g_{(\tau)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+2\mu\|h_{(\tau)}\|^{2}_{2}.

Clearly
W(\tau)
is quadratic in
\tau
and strongly convex. By Lemmas
\thechapter.C.5
and
\thechapter.D.3
,

\displaystyle W(\tau)

\displaystyle=L^{\prime}_{1}\{g_{(\tau)}\}+2\mu\|g_{(\tau)}\|^{2}_{2}+L_{1}\{h_{(\tau)},g_{(\tau)}\}+2\mu\|h_{(\tau)}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle=\sup_{f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{g_{(\tau)}(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})-f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})^{2}\right]+2\mu^{\prime}\|g_{(\tau)}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h_{(\tau)}(B)-g_{(\tau)}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]+2\mu\|h_{(\tau)}\|^{2}_{2}

which is minimized at
\tau=0
by the definition of
\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}
. Therefore by an exact Taylor expansion,

\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}^{2}W(0)=\partial_{\tau}W(0)+\frac{1}{2}\partial_{\tau}^{2}W(0)=W(1)-W(0).

The derivatives are

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}W(\tau)

\displaystyle=2\langle S\{g_{(\tau)}-g_{0}\},S\{\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\rangle_{2}+4\mu^{\prime}\langle g_{(\tau)},\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\rangle_{2}

\displaystyle+2\langle T\{h_{(\tau)}-h_{0},g_{(\tau)}-g_{0}\},T\{\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\rangle_{2}+4\mu\langle h_{(\tau)},\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\rangle_{2},

\displaystyle\partial_{\tau}^{2}W(\tau)

\displaystyle=2\|S\{\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}^{2}+4\mu^{\prime}\|\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\|T\{\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}^{2}+4\mu\|\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}^{2}.

Substituting in
\partial_{\tau}^{2}W(0)
,
W(1)
, and
W(0)
into the Taylor expansion yields the result.
∎

Lemma
\thechapter
.D.5

(Relating weak metrics)
.

Suppose the conditions of Lemmas
\thechapter.C.5
,
\thechapter.D.3
, and
\thechapter.D.1
hold. With probability
1-\zeta
, when
\delta_{n}=\Omega[\{\log\log(n)+\log(1/\zeta)\}^{1/2}n^{-1/2}]
,

\displaystyle\|S(\hat{g}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}-\|S(g_{*}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0},\hat{g}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}-\|T(h_{*}-h_{0},g_{*}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{2}\|S(g_{*}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{3}{2}\|T(h_{*}-h_{0},g_{*}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+2\mu^{\prime}(\|g_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{g}\|^{2}_{2,n})+2\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})

\displaystyle+O\{\delta_{n}\|S(\hat{g}-g_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*},\hat{g}-g_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.

Proof.

We proceed in steps.

1.

By Lemma
\thechapter.D.3
,

\|S(\hat{g}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}=L^{\prime}_{1}(\hat{g})=\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\hat{g}(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}_{\hat{g}}(C^{\prime})-f^{\prime}_{\hat{g}}(C^{\prime})^{2}\right].

By Lemma
\thechapter.C.5
,

\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0},\hat{g}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}=L_{1}(\hat{h},\hat{g})=\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}(f_{\hat{h}}+f_{\hat{g}})(C)-(f_{\hat{h}}+f_{\hat{g}})^{2}(C)\right].

2.

By Lemma
\thechapter.D.1
with probability
1-\zeta
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\hat{g}(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}_{\hat{g}}(C^{\prime})-f^{\prime}_{\hat{g}}(C^{\prime})^{2}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{g}(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}_{\hat{g}}(C^{\prime})-f^{\prime}_{\hat{g}}(C^{\prime})^{2}\right]+O\left(\delta_{n}\|f^{\prime}_{\hat{g}}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}(f_{\hat{h}}+f_{\hat{g}})(C)-(f_{\hat{h}}+f_{\hat{g}})^{2}(C)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}(f_{\hat{h}}+f_{\hat{g}})(C)-(f_{\hat{h}}+f_{\hat{g}})^{2}(C)\right]+O\left(\delta_{n}\|f_{\hat{h}}+f_{\hat{g}}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\right).

3.

By Assumptions
3
and
5
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{g}(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}_{\hat{g}}(C^{\prime})-f^{\prime}_{\hat{g}}(C^{\prime})^{2}\right]\leq\sup_{f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{g}(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})-f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}(f_{\hat{h}}+f_{\hat{g}})(C)-(f_{\hat{h}}+f_{\hat{g}})^{2}(C)\right]\leq\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right].

4.

By the definition of
(\hat{g},\hat{h})
, Lemma
\thechapter.D.1
, AM-GM inequality, and Lemmas
\thechapter.C.5
and
\thechapter.D.3
, with probability
1-\zeta
,

\displaystyle\sup_{f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{g}(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})-f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})^{2}\right]+\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{\hat{h}(B)-\hat{g}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{g_{*}(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})-f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})^{2}\right]+\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}_{n}\left[2\left\{h_{*}(B)-g_{*}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mu^{\prime}(\|g_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{g}\|^{2}_{2,n})+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{g_{*}(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})-f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})^{2}\right]+\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h_{*}(B)-g_{*}(A)\right\}f(C)-f(C)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mu^{\prime}(\|g_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{g}\|^{2}_{2,n})+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})+O(\delta_{n}\|f^{\prime}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|f\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2})

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{g_{*}(A)-Y\right\}f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})-\frac{1}{2}f^{\prime}(C^{\prime})^{2}\right]+\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[2\left\{h_{*}(B)-g_{*}(A)\right\}f(C)-\frac{1}{2}f(C)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\mu^{\prime}(\|g_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{g}\|^{2}_{2,n})+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})+O(\delta_{n}^{2})

\displaystyle=L^{\prime}_{1/2}(g_{*})+L_{1/2}(h_{*},g_{*})+\mu^{\prime}(\|g_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{g}\|^{2}_{2,n})+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})+O(\delta_{n}^{2})

\displaystyle=2\|S(g_{*}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+2\|T(h_{*}-h_{0},g_{*}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+\mu^{\prime}(\|g_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{g}\|^{2}_{2,n})+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})+O(\delta_{n}^{2}).

5.

By Lemmas
\thechapter.C.5
and
\thechapter.D.3
, triangle inequality, and AM-GM inequality,

\displaystyle O(\delta_{n}\|f^{\prime}_{\hat{g}}\|_{2})

\displaystyle=O\{\delta_{n}\|S(\hat{g}-g_{0})\|_{2}\}=O\{\delta_{n}\|S(\hat{g}-g_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|S(g_{*}-g_{0})\|_{2}\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|S(g_{*}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+O\{\delta_{n}\|S(\hat{g}-g_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\},

\displaystyle O(\delta_{n}\|f_{\hat{h}}+f_{\hat{g}}\|_{2})

\displaystyle=O\{\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0},\hat{g}-g_{0})\|_{2}\}

\displaystyle=O\{\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*},\hat{g}-g_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(h_{*}-h_{0},g_{*}-g_{0})\|_{2}\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|T(h_{*}-h_{0},g_{*}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+O\{\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*},\hat{g}-g_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}.

6.

Collecting results,

\displaystyle\|S(\hat{g}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0},\hat{g}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|S(g_{*}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+2\|T(h_{*}-h_{0},g_{*}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+\mu^{\prime}(\|g_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{g}\|^{2}_{2,n})+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})+O(\delta_{n}^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\|S(g_{*}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+O\{\delta_{n}\|S(\hat{g}-g_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\|T(h_{*}-h_{0},g_{*}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+O\{\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*},\hat{g}-g_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}

\displaystyle=\frac{5}{2}\|S(g_{*}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{5}{2}\|T(h_{*}-h_{0},g_{*}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}+\mu^{\prime}(\|g_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{g}\|^{2}_{2,n})+\mu(\|h_{*}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n})

\displaystyle\quad+O(\delta_{n}\|S(\hat{g}-g_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|T(\hat{h}-h_{*},\hat{g}-g_{*})\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2})\qed.

Lemma
\thechapter
.D.6

(Regularization bias)
.

Suppose Assumptions
2
,
4
,
6
,
7
,
8
, and
\thechapter.A.1
hold. Then

\displaystyle\|h_{\mu^{\prime},\mu}-h_{0}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h},1)}+\|w_{g}\|^{2}_{2}\mu^{-1}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}+1,2)}\right\}

\displaystyle\|T_{h}(h_{\mu^{\prime},\mu}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}\|^{2}_{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}+1,2)}\right\}

\displaystyle\|g_{\mu^{\prime},\mu}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}^{\prime}\|^{2}_{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}^{\prime},1)}\right\}

\displaystyle\|S(g_{\mu^{\prime},\mu}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}^{\prime}\|^{2}_{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}^{\prime}+1,2)}\right\}

\displaystyle\|T_{g}(g_{\mu^{\prime},\mu}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}\|^{2}_{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}+1,2)}\right\}.

Proof.

Appendix
\thechapter.E
provides the proof, which is quite involved and invokes the assumption discussed in Appendix
\thechapter.A
. Our argument develops what appear to be new techniques for the nested NPIV problem.
∎

Proof of Theorem
2
.

Take
(g_{*},h_{*})=\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}
.

1.

By Lemmas
\thechapter.D.4
,
\thechapter.D.5
,
\thechapter.C.4
and
\thechapter.D.2
, AM-GM inequality, and
\mu^{\prime},\mu=O(1)
, we bound

\displaystyle\|S\{\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}^{2}+2\mu^{\prime}\|\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}^{2}+\|T\{\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}^{2}+2\mu\|\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|S(\hat{g}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}-\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+2\mu^{\prime}\{\|\hat{g}\|^{2}_{2}-\|g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|^{2}_{2}\}

\displaystyle+\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0},\hat{g}-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}-\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+2\mu\{\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2}-\|h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|^{2}_{2}\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{2}\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{3}{2}\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+2\mu^{\prime}\{\|g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{g}\|^{2}_{2,n}+\|\hat{g}\|^{2}_{2}-\|g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|^{2}_{2}\}

\displaystyle+2\mu\{\|h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|^{2}_{2,n}-\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2,n}+\|\hat{h}\|^{2}_{2}-\|h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|^{2}_{2}\}

\displaystyle+O[\delta_{n}\|S\{\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|T\{\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{2}\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{3}{2}\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle-I_{n}^{\prime}+I^{\prime}-I_{n}+I+O[\delta_{n}\|S\{\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|T\{\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{2}\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{3}{2}\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+O\bigg[\sqrt{\mu^{\prime}}^{2}\delta_{n}\|\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}+\sqrt{\mu}^{2}\delta_{n}\|\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\delta_{n}\|S\{\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}\|T\{\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}+\delta_{n}^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{2}\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{3}{2}\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mu^{\prime}}{2}\|\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{\mu}{2}\|\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\|S\{\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|T\{\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|^{2}_{2}+O(\delta_{n}^{2}).

Rearranging yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|S\{\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|T\{\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\mu^{\prime}}{2}\|\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{3\mu}{2}\|\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{2}\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+\frac{3}{2}\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+O(\delta_{n}^{2}).

Therefore
\|S\{\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}^{2}
and
\|T\{\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}^{2}
are each bounded by

\displaystyle 3\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+3\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+O(\delta_{n}^{2});

\displaystyle\|\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}/\mu^{\prime}+\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}/\mu^{\prime}+O(\delta_{n}^{2}/\mu^{\prime});

\displaystyle\|\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\mu^{-1}\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+\mu^{-1}\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+O(\mu^{-1}\delta_{n}^{2}).

2.

For the weak metric result, we use triangle inequality and Lemma
\thechapter.D.6
. For
R_{n}=\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}^{\prime}\|^{2}_{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}^{\prime}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}\|^{2}_{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}+1,2)}+\delta_{n}^{2}
,

\displaystyle\|S(\hat{g}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}\leq 2\|S\{\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}^{2}+2\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 8\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+6\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+O(\delta_{n}^{2})

\displaystyle=O\{\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}^{\prime}\|^{2}_{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}^{\prime}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}\|^{2}_{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}+1,2)}+\delta_{n}^{2}\}=O(R_{n}),

\displaystyle\|T(\hat{h}-h_{0},\hat{g}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}\leq 2\|T\{\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)},\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\}\|_{2}^{2}+2\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 6\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+8\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+O(\delta_{n}^{2})=O(R_{n}).

3.

For the strong metric result, we use triangle inequality and Lemma
\thechapter.D.6
:

\displaystyle\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\leq 2\|\hat{g}-g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}^{2}+2\|g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2(\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+2(\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+2\|g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+O\{(\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\delta_{n}^{2}\}

\displaystyle=O\left\{(\mu^{\prime})^{-1}R_{n}+\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}^{\prime}\|^{2}_{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}^{\prime},1)}+(\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\delta_{n}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=O\left\{(\mu^{\prime})^{-1}R_{n}\right\},

\displaystyle\|\hat{h}-h_{0}\|_{2}^{2}\leq 2\|\hat{h}-h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}\|_{2}^{2}+2\|h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\mu^{-1}\|S\{g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}+2\mu^{-1}\|T\{h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0},g_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-g_{0}\}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle+2\|h_{(2\mu^{\prime},2\mu)}-h_{0}\|_{2}^{2}+O(\mu^{-1}\delta_{n}^{2})

\displaystyle=O\left\{\mu^{-1}R_{n}+\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h},1)}+\|w_{g}\|^{2}_{2}\mu^{-1}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}+1,2)}+\mu^{-1}\delta_{n}^{2}\right\}=O(\mu^{-1}R_{n}).

4.

In particular, when
\mu=\mu^{\prime}
,
\|\hat{g}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}=O(\mu^{-1}R_{n})
and
\|\hat{h}-h_{0}\|_{2}^{2}=O(\mu^{-1}R_{n})
where

R_{n}=\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}^{\prime}\|^{2}_{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{g}^{\prime}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}\|^{2}_{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{g}+1,2)}+\delta_{n}^{2}.\qed

Appendix
\thechapter
.E
New techniques to control bias

We control the bias of Algorithm
1
using standard techniques. However, to control the bias of Algorithm
2
, we develop what appear to be new techniques. To lighten notation, we abbreviate
\mu I=\mu
and
\mu^{\prime}I=\mu^{\prime}
when it is clear from context.

\thechapter
.E.1
Algorithm
1

Lemma
\thechapter
.E.1

(Bias algebra; c.f. Lemma 3 of
Bennett et al., 2023c
)
.

For any regularization parameter value
\mu>0
, source parameter value
\beta>0
, and singular values
(\sigma_{j})
satisfying
\sup_{j}\sigma_{j}\leq 1
, we have that

\mu^{2}\sup_{j}\frac{\sigma_{j}^{2\beta}}{(\sigma_{j}^{2}+\mu)^{2}}\leq\mu^{\min(\beta,2)}

and
\mu^{2}\sup_{j}\frac{\sigma_{j}^{2(\beta+1)}}{(\sigma_{j}^{2}+\mu)^{2}}\leq\mu^{\min(\beta+1,2)}
.

Proof.

We state the proof for completeness.

1.

For the first result, if
\beta\geq 2
then it suffices to show
\sup_{j}\frac{\sigma_{j}^{2\beta}}{(\sigma_{j}^{2}+\mu)^{2}}\leq 1
. Clearly
\frac{\sigma_{j}^{2\beta}}{(\sigma_{j}^{2}+\mu)^{2}}\leq\frac{\sigma_{j}^{2\beta}}{\sigma_{j}^{4}}=\sigma_{j}^{2(\beta-2)}\leq 1
since
\beta\geq 2
and
\sup_{j}\sigma_{j}\leq 1
. If
\beta<2
then it suffices to show
f(x)=\mu^{2}\frac{x^{\beta}}{(x+\mu)^{2}}\leq\mu^{\beta}
. The first order condition yields
x_{*}=\frac{\beta\mu}{2-\beta}
and
f(x_{*})=\frac{1}{4}(2-\beta)^{2-\beta}\beta^{\beta}\mu^{\beta}\leq\mu^{\beta}
since
\beta<2
.

2.

For the second result, if
\beta+1\geq 2
then it suffices to show
\sup_{j}\frac{\sigma_{j}^{2(\beta+1)}}{(\sigma_{j}^{2}+\mu)^{2}}\leq 1
. Clearly
\frac{\sigma_{j}^{2(\beta+1)}}{(\sigma_{j}^{2}+\mu)^{2}}\leq\frac{\sigma_{j}^{2(\beta+1)}}{\sigma_{j}^{4}}=\sigma_{j}^{2(\beta+1-2)}\leq 1
since
\beta\geq 1
and
\sup_{j}\sigma_{j}\leq 1
. If
\beta+1<2
then it suffices to show
f(x)=\mu^{2}\frac{x^{(\beta+1)}}{(x+\mu)^{2}}\leq\mu^{\beta+1}
. The first order condition yields
x_{*}=\frac{(\beta+1)\mu}{1-\beta}
and
f(x_{*})=\frac{1}{4}(1-\beta)^{1-\beta}(\beta+1)^{\beta+1}\mu^{\beta+1}\leq\mu^{\beta+1}
since
\beta<1
. ∎

Proof of Lemma
\thechapter.C.9
.

We state the proof for completeness. To lighten notation, we study
h_{\mu}=h_{*}
and abbreviate
T_{h}=T
.

1.

By Lemma
\thechapter.C.5
,

h_{*}=\argmin_{h\in\mathcal{H}}L_{1}(h,g_{0})+\mu\|h\|_{2}^{2}=\|T(h-h_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+\mu\|h\|_{2}^{2}.

Taking the Gateaux derivative, the first order condition yields
2T^{*}T(h_{*}-h_{0})+2\mu h_{*}=0
and hence
h_{*}=(T^{*}T+\mu)^{-1}(T^{*}T)h_{0}
. Hence

h_{*}-h_{0}=(T^{*}T+\mu)^{-1}\{(T^{*}T)-(T^{*}T+\mu)\}h_{0}=-\mu(T^{*}T+\mu)^{-1}h_{0}.

Using
h_{0}=(T^{*}T)^{\beta/2}w_{0}
,

\displaystyle\|h_{*}-h_{0}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|-\mu(T^{*}T+\mu)^{-1}(T^{*}T)^{\beta/2}w_{0}\|^{2}_{2}\leq\mu^{2}\|(T^{*}T+\mu)^{-1}(T^{*}T)^{\beta/2}\|^{2}_{\text{op}}\cdot\|w_{0}\|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\|T(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|-\mu T(T^{*}T+\mu)^{-1}(T^{*}T)^{\beta/2}w_{0}\|^{2}_{2}\leq\mu^{2}\|T(T^{*}T+\mu)^{-1}(T^{*}T)^{\beta/2}\|^{2}_{\text{op}}\cdot\|w_{0}\|_{2}^{2}.

2.

By Lemma
\thechapter.E.1
,
\mu^{2}\|(T^{*}T+\mu)^{-1}(T^{*}T)^{\beta/2}\|^{2}_{\text{op}}=\mu^{2}\sup_{j}\frac{\sigma_{j}^{2\beta}}{(\sigma_{j}^{2}+\mu)^{2}}\leq\mu^{\min(\beta,2)}
and
\mu^{2}\|T(T^{*}T+\mu)^{-1}(T^{*}T)^{\beta/2}\|^{2}_{\text{op}}=\mu^{2}\sup_{j}\frac{\sigma_{j}^{2(\beta+1)}}{(\sigma_{j}^{2}+\mu)^{2}}\leq\mu^{\min(\beta+1,2)}
. ∎

\thechapter
.E.2
Algorithm
2

To lighten notation, we write
(g_{*},h_{*})=(g_{\mu^{\prime},\mu},h_{\mu^{\prime},\mu})
. We also abbreviate
T_{h}=T(h,0)
and
T_{g}=T(0,g)
, so that
T(h,g)=T_{h}(h)+T_{g}(g)
.

Lemma
\thechapter
.E.2

(Rewriting regularization bias)
.

We have

g_{*}-g_{0}=(I-A_{G}A_{H})^{-1}(\tilde{g}-A_{G}\tilde{h}),\quad h_{*}-h_{0}=(I-A_{H}A_{G})^{-1}(\tilde{h}-A_{H}\tilde{g})

where

\displaystyle\tilde{g}=-\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}g_{0},\quad\tilde{h}=-\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}h_{0},

\displaystyle A_{G}=(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}T_{h},\quad A_{H}=(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}T_{h}^{*}T_{g}.

If Assumptions
2
,
4
, and
8
hold then

\displaystyle\tilde{g}

\displaystyle=-\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}w_{g}^{\prime}=-\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}w_{g}

\displaystyle\tilde{h}

\displaystyle=-\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}w_{h}.

Proof.

By Lemmas
\thechapter.C.5
and
\thechapter.D.3
,

\displaystyle(g_{*},h_{*})=\argmin_{g\in\mathcal{G},h\in\mathcal{H}}L^{\prime}_{1}(g)+L_{1}(h,g)+\mu^{\prime}\|g\|_{2}^{2}+\mu\|h\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|S(g-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+\|T(h-h_{0},g-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+\mu^{\prime}\|g\|_{2}^{2}+\mu\|h\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|S(g-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+\|T_{h}(h-h_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+\|T_{g}(g-g_{0})\|^{2}_{2}+2\langle T_{h}(h-h_{0}),T_{g}(g-g_{0})\rangle+\mu^{\prime}\|g\|_{2}^{2}+\mu\|h\|_{2}^{2}.

Taking the Gateaux derivative, the first order conditions yield, after dividing by two,

(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g})(g_{*}-g_{0})+T_{g}^{*}T_{h}(h_{*}-h_{0})+\mu^{\prime}g_{*}=0,\quad T_{h}^{*}T_{h}(h_{*}-h_{0})+T_{h}^{*}T_{g}(g_{*}-g_{0})+\mu h_{*}=0.

Rearranging each expression,

\displaystyle g_{*}

\displaystyle=(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\{(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g})g_{0}-T_{g}^{*}T_{h}(h_{*}-h_{0})\},

\displaystyle h_{*}

\displaystyle=(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\{T_{h}^{*}T_{h}h_{0}-T_{h}^{*}T_{g}(g_{*}-g_{0})\};

\displaystyle g_{*}-g_{0}

\displaystyle=(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\{-\mu^{\prime}g_{0}-T_{g}^{*}T_{h}(h_{*}-h_{0})\}=\tilde{g}-A_{G}(h_{*}-h_{0}),

\displaystyle h_{*}-h_{0}

\displaystyle=(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\{-\mu h_{0}-T_{h}^{*}T_{g}(g_{*}-g_{0})\}=\tilde{h}-A_{H}(g_{*}-g_{0}).

Combining the final two expressions yields the former the result. The latter result is immediate.
∎

For operators
A
and
B
, we write
A\geq B
in the Loewner sense when
A-B
is positive semidefinite.

Lemma
\thechapter
.E.3

(Rewriting pre-factors)
.

We have

\displaystyle(I-A_{G}A_{H})^{-1}=(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})

\displaystyle(I-A_{H}A_{G})^{-1}=\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu).

for some
R\geq 0
and some
Q\geq 0
satisfying
I-Q\geq cI
with
c=\|(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{1/2}(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{1/2}\|_{\text{op}}^{-1}
.

Proof.

Since
B^{-1}=(AB)^{-1}A
, by Lemma
\thechapter.E.5
,

\displaystyle(I-A_{G}A_{H})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\{I-(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}T_{h}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}T_{h}^{*}T_{g}\}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\{S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime}-T_{g}^{*}T_{h}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}T_{h}^{*}T_{g}\}^{-1}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\{S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}IT_{g}+\mu^{\prime}-T_{g}^{*}T_{h}T_{h}^{*}(T_{h}T_{h}^{*}+\mu)^{-1}T_{g}\}^{-1}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\{S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}\mu(T_{h}T_{h}^{*}+\mu)^{-1}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime}\}^{-1}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime}).

We take
R=T_{g}^{*}\mu(T_{h}T_{h}^{*}+\mu)^{-1}T_{g}\geq 0
. Similarly,

\displaystyle(I-A_{H}A_{G})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\{I-(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}T_{h}^{*}T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}T_{h}\}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\{T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu-T_{h}^{*}T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}T_{h}\}^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)

\displaystyle=\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)

where
Q=T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}\geq 0
.

Finally, we characterize
c
. By
Bhatia, 2013
,
\sigma_{j}(A^{*}B)\leq\frac{1}{2}\sigma_{j}(AA^{*}+BB^{*})
. Taking
A^{*}=T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1/2}
and
B=(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1/2}T_{g}^{*}
, we have that for each
j
,

\displaystyle\sigma_{j}(Q)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{j}\{(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1/2}T_{g}^{*}T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1/2}\}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{j}\{I-(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1/2}(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1/2}\}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{j}[I-\{(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{1/2}(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{1/2}\}^{-1}]

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{1}[I-\{(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{1/2}(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{1/2}\}^{-1}]

\displaystyle=1-\frac{1}{\sigma_{1}\{(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{1/2}(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{1/2}\}}=1-c

for
c=\|(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{1/2}(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{1/2}\|_{\text{op}}^{-1}
. Therefore
Q\leq(1-c)I
in the Loewner sense.
∎

Assumption
\thechapter
.E.1

(High level conditions)
.

Suppose that the following conditions hold:

1.

\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}\|_{\text{op}}=O(1)
;

2.

\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\|_{\text{op}}=O\{(\mu^{\prime})^{-1/2}\}
;

3.

\|T_{h}\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}T_{h}^{*}\|_{\text{op}}=O(1)
;

4.

\|T_{h}\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}\|_{\text{op}}=O(\mu^{-1/2})
;

5.

\|\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}T_{h}^{*}\|_{\text{op}}=O(\mu^{-1/2})
;

6.

\|\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}\|_{\text{op}}=O(\mu^{-1})
;

7.

\|S(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}S^{*}\|_{\text{op}}=O(1)
;

8.

\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}\|_{\text{op}}=O(1)
;

9.

\|S(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\|_{\text{op}}=O\{(\mu^{\prime})^{-1/2}\}
;

10.

\|(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}S^{*}\|_{\text{op}}=O\{(\mu^{\prime})^{-1/2}\}
;

11.

\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\|_{\text{op}}=O\{(\mu^{\prime})^{-1/2}\}
;

12.

\|(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}\|_{\text{op}}=O\{(\mu^{\prime})^{-1/2}\}
;

13.

\|S(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}\|_{\text{op}}=O(1)
;

14.

\|(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\|_{\text{op}}=O\{(\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\}
.

Lemma
\thechapter
.E.4
.

Suppose Assumptions
2
,
4
,
8
, and
\thechapter.E.1
hold. Then

\displaystyle\|h_{*}-h_{0}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h},1)}+\|w_{g}\|^{2}_{2}\mu^{-1}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}+1,2)}\right\}

\displaystyle\|T_{h}(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}\|^{2}_{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}+1,2)}\right\}

\displaystyle\|g_{*}-g_{0}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}^{\prime}\|^{2}_{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}^{\prime},1)}\right\}

\displaystyle\|S(g_{*}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}^{\prime}\|^{2}_{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}^{\prime}+1,2)}\right\}

\displaystyle\|T_{g}(g_{*}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|w_{h}\|_{2}^{2}\mu^{\min(\beta_{h}+1,2)}+\|w_{g}\|^{2}_{2}(\mu^{\prime})^{\min(\beta_{g}+1,2)}\right\}.

Proof.

To lighten notation, we abbreviate
\|\cdot\|=\|\cdot\|_{\text{op}}
.

1.

By Lemma
\thechapter.E.2
,
\tilde{g}=-G^{\prime}w_{g}^{\prime}=-Gw_{g}
and
\tilde{h}=-Hw_{h}
where

\displaystyle G^{\prime}=\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2},\quad G=\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}

\displaystyle H=\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}.

2.

To bound
\|h_{*}-h_{0}\|^{2}_{2}
, it suffices to control
\|(I-A_{H}A_{G})^{-1}H\|
and
\|(I-A_{H}A_{G})^{-1}A_{H}G\|
by Lemma
\thechapter.E.2
and the characterization above. By Lemma
\thechapter.E.3
and triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\|(I-A_{H}A_{G})^{-1}H\|=\|\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle=\|\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}T_{h}^{*}T_{h}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}T_{h}^{*}\|\|T_{h}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\mu\|\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}\|\|(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle=O\left\{\mu^{-1/2}\|T_{h}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|+\|(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|\right\}

where in the last step we use Assumptions
\thechapter.E.1
.5 and
\thechapter.E.1
.6. After squaring, Lemma
\thechapter.E.1
gives the first term in the bound.

Next we turn to

\displaystyle\|(I-A_{H}A_{G})^{-1}A_{H}G\|=\|\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}T_{h}^{*}T_{g}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}T_{h}^{*}\|\|T_{g}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|.

The former factor is
O(\mu^{-1/2})
by Assumption
\thechapter.E.1
.5. We bound the latter factor as

\displaystyle\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle=\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}T_{g}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}\|\|T_{g}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\mu^{\prime}\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\|\|(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|T_{g}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|+(\mu^{\prime})^{1/2}\|(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|\right\}

where in the last step we use Assumptions
\thechapter.E.1
.1 and
\thechapter.E.1
.2. After squaring, Lemma
\thechapter.E.1
gives the second term in the bound.

3.

The argument for
\|T_{h}(h_{*}-h_{0})\|_{2}^{2}
is similar. First we control

\displaystyle\|T_{h}(I-A_{H}A_{G})^{-1}H\|=\|T_{h}\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle=\|T_{h}\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|T_{h}\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}T_{h}^{*}T_{h}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|T_{h}\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|T_{h}\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}T_{h}^{*}\|\|T_{h}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\mu\|T_{h}\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}\|\|(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|T_{h}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|+\mu^{1/2}\|(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|\right\}

where in the last step we use Assumptions
\thechapter.E.1
.3 and
\thechapter.E.1
.4. After squaring, Lemma
\thechapter.E.1
gives the first term in the bound.
Next we study

\displaystyle\|T_{h}(I-A_{H}A_{G})^{-1}A_{H}G\|

\displaystyle=\|T_{h}\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}T_{h}^{*}T_{g}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|T_{h}\{T_{h}^{*}(I-Q)T_{h}+\mu\}^{-1}T_{h}^{*}\|\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|.

The former factor is
O(1)
by Assumption
\thechapter.E.1
.3. We bound the latter factor as above, which gives the second term in the bound.

4.

To bound
\|g_{*}-g_{0}\|^{2}_{2}
, it suffices to control
\|(I-A_{G}A_{H})^{-1}G^{\prime}\|
and
\|(I-A_{G}A_{H})^{-1}A_{G}H\|
by Lemma
\thechapter.E.2
and the characterization above. By Lemma
\thechapter.E.3
and triangle inequality,

\displaystyle\|(I-A_{G}A_{H})^{-1}G^{\prime}\|=\|(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle=\|(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}S^{*}S(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}S^{*}\|\|S(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\mu^{\prime}\|(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\|\|(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle=O\left\{(\mu^{\prime})^{-1/2}\|S(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|+\|(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|\right\}

where in the last step we use Assumptions
\thechapter.E.1
.10 and
\thechapter.E.1
.14. After squaring, Lemma
\thechapter.E.1
gives the second term in the bound.

Next we turn to

\displaystyle\|(I-A_{G}A_{H})^{-1}A_{G}H\|=\|(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}T_{h}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}\|\|T_{h}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle=O\left\{\right(\mu^{\prime})^{-1/2}\|T_{h}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|\}

where in the last line we use Assumption
\thechapter.E.1
.12. After squaring, Lemma
\thechapter.E.1
gives the first term in the bound.

5.

The argument for
\|S(g_{*}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}
is similar. First we control

\displaystyle\|S(I-A_{G}A_{H})^{-1}G^{\prime}\|=\|S(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle=\|S(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|S(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}S^{*}S(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|S(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|S(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}S^{*}\|\|S(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\mu^{\prime}\|S(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\|\|(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|S(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|+(\mu^{\prime})^{1/2}\|(S^{*}S+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(S^{*}S)^{\beta^{\prime}_{g}/2}\|\right\}

where in the last line we use Assumptions
\thechapter.E.1
.7 and
\thechapter.E.1
.9. After squaring, Lemma
\thechapter.E.1
gives the second term in the bound.
Next we turn to

\displaystyle\|S(I-A_{G}A_{H})^{-1}A_{G}H\|=\|S(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}T_{h}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|S(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}\|\|T_{h}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle=O\{\|T_{h}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|\}

where in the last line we use Assumption
\thechapter.E.1
.13. After squaring, Lemma
\thechapter.E.1
gives the first term in the bound.

6.

The argument for
\|T_{g}(g_{*}-g_{0})\|_{2}^{2}
is also similar. First we control

\displaystyle\|T_{g}(I-A_{G}A_{H})^{-1}G\|=\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle=\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}T_{g}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}\|\|T_{g}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\mu^{\prime}\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\|\|(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|T_{g}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|+(\mu^{\prime})^{1/2}\|(T_{g}^{*}T_{g}+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}\mu^{\prime}(T_{g}^{*}T_{g})^{\beta_{g}/2}\|\right\}

where in the last line we use Assumptions
\thechapter.E.1
.8 and
\thechapter.E.1
.11. After squaring, Lemma
\thechapter.E.1
gives the second term in the bound.

Finally, we turn to

\displaystyle\|T_{g}(I-A_{G}A_{H})^{-1}A_{G}H\|=\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}T_{h}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|T_{g}(S^{*}S+R+\mu^{\prime})^{-1}T_{g}^{*}\|\|T_{h}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|

\displaystyle=O\left\{\|T_{h}\mu(T_{h}^{*}T_{h}+\mu)^{-1}(T_{h}^{*}T_{h})^{\beta_{h}/2}\|\right\}

where in the last line we use Assumption
\thechapter.E.1
.8. After squaring, Lemma
\thechapter.E.1
gives the first term in the bound. ∎

Proof of Lemma
\thechapter.D.6
.

The result is an immediate consequence of Lemmas
\thechapter.E.4
and

. The proof of Lemma

is given below.
